=== That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/For-Love-of-the-Game
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Sports Teams; Indian Institute of Techn...; Campus Life; Table Tennis; Sporting Events; Inter IIT Sports Meet; Team Culture; Sports Venues; Student Life at IIT Kanpu...; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 23.

Mugdha Arora
B.Tech in Electrical Engineering, Indian Institute of Technology, Kanpur (IITK) (Graduated 2019)8y
For Love of the Game

Hello folks! Congratulations on making it through the “big” exam of your life! Tough choices ahead, right? Well I am pretty sure you wouldn’t want to make them based on anything you are about to (if you choose to) read ahead. Happy reading nonetheless!

July 2015- It’s probably the Day 2 of Orientation (read how awesome this is in other articles) and I get a call from the Captain of Girls Table Tennis Team. She asks me if I could see her on the court anytime in the evening.

December 2017- I am heading off home from Pondicherry which I visited with my TT team after I played my second Inter IIT at Madras. I did have the time of my life.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
June 2018- I just finished my third year. I am completely clueless on how to choose between an MS and a job. And I am about to call the Captain from my first year for advice after I finish this article. (And interestingly, the second person I plan to call is the former Boys TT Team Captain)

You can never fathom all the love and guidance being on any team has to offer unless you have been there. I’ll get back to this lovely experience later. Let’s begin with the more ‘factual description’.

I wouldn’t step back from boasting about all that IITK has to offer for the sports enthusiasts. We have an olympic-sized 8 lane swimming pool, 400m running track, throwing arena with cage, jumping pit, 5 wooden Badminton courts, 4 Basketball courts, 2 Cricket grounds, a Football ground with floodlights, a Hockey ground with floodlights, 6 Lawn Tennis courts, 4 wooden Squash courts, a wooden Table Tennis court with 9 tables and a robot, 5 Volleyball courts, Weightlifting facilities, Wall Climbing arena, Skating rink, Pool tables and a huge Yoga hall. Apart from this almost all of the 13 hostels have Badminton, Volleyball, Basketball, Cricket and Football facilities.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
Our Games and Sports Council, a student run body in coordination with Sports and Physical Education Committee (SPEC), is the biggest council on campus in terms of infrastructure and people involved both. We have 19 institute teams (with 1 or more coaches for each sport) participating in the Inter IIT Sports Meet forming a contingent of 156 participating students and many others who would make it to the teams in the coming years. We have 6 clubs (Adventure, Card and Board Games, Chess, Shooting, Skating, Taekwondo) and 2 hobby groups (Bicycling and Boxing) organizing some of the best events year long. We also have a Sports Secretary in each of the halls (hostels) looking after intra-hall sports facilities and inter-hall events.
Team Culture
Sports Venues

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
The campus provides numerous opportunities to participate in sports events- Freshers’ Inferno (Inter-Hall Sports Meet for freshers), Main Inferno (Inter-Hall Sports Meet for all), Josh (Informal Sports Meet- open to all ), Enthusia (PG Sports Meet) and Udghosh (Sports festival of IIT Kanpur- one of the biggest in India). Apart from these, we have several workshops and marathons round the year. We participate in Inter IIT Sports Meet every year and some of the sports festivals of other colleges like Sportech (IITD), Spardha (IIT-BHU), Sangram (IITR), Aahvaan (IITB) etc. Teams also regularly conduct practice matches with other teams in Kanpur or outside.

This was a chunk of all we have to offer and something I can, probably, easily put into words. The more difficult part is giving an insight of the sports culture here. Well to begin with, we have our Director and DoSA (Dean of Students Affairs) immensely enthused about the Inter IIT Sports Meet and see them tracking our practice and performance each year. You regularly see professors on courts playing with students.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
The sight of players sweating it out on fields and courts two times a day everyday is one to behold. There are some teams coming for a brief practice session even during the exams. And no, this is not even a slight manifestation of a callous attitude towards academics. We are as motivated towards academics as one could be. But it is all about the fact that sports teaches us a disciplined way of life and we stick to it throughout. Along with the regular practice sessions, Summer Camp (during Summer Break) is yet another opportunity that we offer to players (mostly freshmen) to hone their talent and spend a productive summers on campus.

We have been Inter IIT Champions for three consecutive years (2013, 2014, 2016)* and stood third in the 52nd edition at Madras in 2017; along with other great feats. Our performance in the past few years has been a clear manifestation of our hard work as well as the support and appreciation sports receives on campus. We have a Sports Felicitation Ceremony every year to congratulate players and teams for their efforts and achievements.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
Fun Fact: If you refer to ‘Inter IIT’, it unequivocally is referring to Inter IIT Sports Meet. Inter IIT Cult Meet and Inter IIT Tech Meet almost always use their full titles.

Coming back to the lovely experience of being a part of the team, we have literally had our team seniors mentoring juniors on night-outs before quizzes. The team becomes your family here at IITK. I, personally, have never felt more attached to a group of people than I have to my team. They are your go-to people all the time. From festivals to tournaments, awesome treats to tiring practice sessions and trips to farewells, we have done it all together. And the best thing about this is that it will stay, forever. I know if I am visiting a city with a team senior there, I don’t have to worry about anything. They’ll have me covered. This bond you share with your team, all the ups and downs, all the motivational speeches you get from seniors and all the fun you have with juniors; this all becomes an integral part of your life here.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
Fun Fact 2: If you aren’t into playing but more of a sports follower, we screen many important matches in the OAT (Open Air Theatre - check out photos on IITK’s website or elsewhere)

Sports at IITK is not only about sincere, dedicated achievers. We have a huge chunk of campus junta playing for leisure and for the plain fun of the game. Intra-hall sports activities, to mention one, are definitely more than what you would have played in school time (since there are no mom-dad and neighbors stopping you to play at midnight). You do not have to worry about equipment (you can issue most of it here) and hence, it’s fun venturing into any number of new sports as well.

Fun Fact 3: If gaming is what you seek, there is no end to LAN gaming people do here.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]
So if you are someone who hasn’t played for years because you were busy preparing for the “big” exam and now you wish to pursue sports because let’s face it, “the days to worry are finally over”. And further, if you feel that the availability of sports opportunities could be one of the factors you could base your “most important decision” on, WELCOME ABOARD!

*Inter IIT 2015 was canceled because of floods in Madras

PS- I don’t endorse views mentioned between double quotes!

Check out the links to know more about sports at IITK:-

https://www.facebook.com/sports.iitk/

http://students.iitk.ac.in/sports/

For Inter IIT:-

https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inter_IIT_Sports_Meet

My journey with Sports at IITK- I have been a part of the Table Tennis Team on campus since my first year. I have been the Captain of the Girls TT Team in my second and third year and I have worked as the Senior Executive of the Games and Sports Council in my third year.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: For Love Of The Game]

=== That's IITK article: From Building Race Cars To Companies Iitk Offers It All ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/From-building-race-cars-to-companies-IITK-offers-it-all
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Engineering; Higher Education in India; Formula SAE; Student Clubs and Activit...; Entrepreneurship; Indian Institute of Techn...; Campus Life; Society of Automotive Eng...; Student Life (general); 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 14.
Student Clubs and Activit...

Purwaj Tiwari
From building race cars to companies, IITK offers it all!

Before I start, if you’re reading this then perhaps you’re one of the brightest minds of the country and if so, congratulations are in order. Kudos on clearing one of the toughest exams of the country. While enjoying the new found pride associated with it, it’s important to set your eyes on the next target and probably that’s the reason why you’re here. When you reach the end of the article I hope that some to those doubts (if not all) will be cleared. There are plenty of articles available online which would help you decide which IIT to choose and which major to look forward to. What I will be focusing on, is how IIT Kanpur would create a nurturing ground to explore your deepest desires and bring out the engineer, the entrepreneur, and the leader that has remained dormant within you.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: From Building Race Cars To Companies Ii]
IIT Kanpur offers plethora of opportunities for students to engage with their interests and test their efficacy to add value to the society. Of all the things that I did at the campus nothing comes close to my experience with Society of Automotive Engineers, IIT Kanpur (SAE IITK). Ten years down the line, if I shall be asked to describe my time at IIT Kanpur, the most vivid description would be the one with SAE IITK. It is the only student body that gives you a chance to implement your engineering skills and knowledge on a real life project in the form of a full scale race car. During my time with SAE IITK, I’ve had the pleasure of working on two different race car concepts, one was an off-road (Baja) vehicle while the other was a Formula type (FSAE). Both these projects had given me a chance understand the very essence and elegance of engineering while using it to solve a real life problem. While working on a project of this scale, where you end up building a ‘real’ race car that can accelerate from 0 – 100 km/h in a breathtaking time (quite literally) of 3 seconds, you accomplish a stupendous feat, achievable exclusively on a project like this one.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: From Building Race Cars To Companies Ii]
“The great thing about FSAE is its full production cycle” said Dolly Singh, the Former Director of SpaceX and this statement highlights the next learning step which is execution of these ideas, transcending the classroom walls and into the real world. This involves skills that include project management, fundraising, team management and the ability to ‘not’ crack under pressure, something that you may face every day when you’re out there. This project in itself is so rigorous that it will give you a feel of how a startup is born, and you will be the spearheading it to overcome the challenges it encounters at every step forward. This gives you a taste of how things unravel in the real world, and empowers you tackle the problems even in situations where they are least expected. The team has played a pivotal role in the birth of startups in the past and would continue to foster budding entrepreneurs in the future.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: From Building Race Cars To Companies Ii]
With that said, if you’re one of the lucky ones, meeting the fitness criteria you can be the next race car driver and experience the thrill and exhilaration of driving the race car on the proving grounds of automotive racing! It will be in your hands to unleash the power of the beast on the race tracks that have been a witness to the music of the screaming F1 engines. More than that, the satisfaction that you achieve while driving a car built by your own hands, seeing thousands of parts working in synergy with one another while you push your car to its limits, truly priceless!

My time with SAE IIT Kanpur (or the team IITK Motorsports) has been the best time I’ve experienced on the campus. The veritable spectrum of memories and experiences associated with the club is something which I shall cherish till the very end, not because it’s something that took most of my time, but because it was something that laid a major impact on my heart and soul. When you attach yourself to a project like FSAE or Baja, the car you so affectionately build and drive becomes an emotion, the people you worked with, become family! So if you look forward to join IITK and become leaders of tomorrow, I wholeheartedly welcome you to the campus on behalf of SAE IIT Kanpur family! All the best for your future!
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: From Building Race Cars To Companies Ii]
#Design_Build_Race

In case you wish to know more, check out the following links and feel free to contact any of the team members.
https://www.iitk.ac.in/ame/sae/
https://www.facebook.com/SAE.IITK/

=== That's IITK article: How Is Life In Iit Kanpur ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/How-is-life-in-IIT-Kanpur
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Higher Education; IITs in India; Guest Lecture; Sports Venues; Life and Living; University of Pennsylvani...; The College and Universit...; Universities and Colleges...; Campus Life; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 9.

Neil Shirude

Former Overall Coordinator, Outreach Cell at Indian Institute of Technology, Kanpur (IITK) (2020–2021)6y

#ThatsIITK

Rajan Singh
CEO HabitStrong, Ex-IPS, IITK, Wharton MBA, McKinsey, PE7y
How is life in IIT Kanpur?

25 years after graduating from IITK, I visited the campus a few days ago for a guest lecture during the entrepreneurship summit.
Sports Venues
Life and Living

Honestly, I did not expect to be surprised - after all, it was not a new place for me. How wrong I was!

The size, scale, and beauty of the campus blew me away.

Forget the academic infrastructure for a minute. The sports facilities are so professional that you would think it is India’s premium sports training facility. Be it football, basketball, hockey, squash, badminton, tennis - you name it and they have just absolutely world-class facilities. There is absolutely nothing more you can ask for.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: How Is Life In Iit Kanpur]
Below are a few photos of the sports facilities I took:

Most of these facilities are new and did not exist during my undergraduate days. What was then a dry wasteland, has been developed into this beautiful infrastructure.

There are more hostels than I care to count. The canteen food is supposed to be very good and cheap. Even the Cafe Coffee Day food is subsidized and much cheaper than outside. The food carts for street food are also very cheap, and super delicious. I had a yummy bhutta for Rs 15 :)

I could not resist a selfie in front of Hall 1, where I spent my final year:

The academic infrastructure is also amazing. There are air-conditioned study lounges, a massive library, and beautiful lecture halls. You really have no excuse for not studying.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: How Is Life In Iit Kanpur]
The campus is lush green, wooded, and full of birds and peacocks. I found the campus richer than even an Ivy League campus like that of University of Pennsylvania, where I went to business school.
The College and Universit...

And in case you are wondering if IITK is all about infrastructure - its academics is generally believed to be the most rigorous among all IITs.

The E-Summit was organized amazingly, and the students are much smarter and savvier than us.

I would strongly urge school students to visit the IITK campus if they get an opportunity. It will inspire them.

Below are two young 2nd-year students I spoke with: Deepankur (Computer Science) and Akarsh (Civil Engineering). I don’t have a photo of Dhruv Mittal (1st year - Mathematics and Computing), who accompanied me from the airport. I would like to thank them for their hospitality.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: How Is Life In Iit Kanpur]
Below are a few more photos I clicked.

The above is the Visitor’s Hostel, where I was staying.

=== That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/IIT-Kanpur-A-Freshmans-Insight
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Indian Institutes of Tech...; Hostel Life; Choosing Colleges; Student Clubs and Activit...; College and University Li...; Student Advice; College Festivals and Eve...; Campus Life; 4 more tags not shown; upvotes: 69; comments: 1.

Kunal Kapila

B.S. in Mathematics & Computer Science, Indian Institute of Technology, Kanpur (IITK) (Graduated 2019)11y
IIT Kanpur: A Freshman's Insight

This is for the young prodigies who have just made it to the set of the top-notch engineering colleges of the country, i.e. the Indian Institutes of Technology, after clearing JEE Advanced 2016. Congratulations! We hope a smile just crossed your face!
Choosing Colleges
Student Clubs and Activit...
Student Advice
College Festivals and Eve...

The last two or three years were enough to put your perseverance, faith, hard work, and abilities to test. Now comes the time to make a life changing decision; this is yet another attempt to measure your decision-making capabilities: the perplexing task of choosing which IIT to head to, which branch to pursue, with a colossal amount of dreams and aspirations in your mind. We, Krutika and Kunal Kapila, two IIT Kanpur sophomores hereby present this article to convince you why Kanpur is the best place for you to spend your next four years!

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
Some of you may be attracted to metro cities like Delhi and Mumbai and trust me, no one can blame you for that. However this place has a charm of its own. Kunal (yup, that’s me), in spite of being a Delhite, cherishes every moment of his life here.

It is said that all good things come to an end, but IIT-K does not! The lush campus is spread over a large area, in an environment suitable enough even for the sensitive peacock to thrive in large numbers. Not a single soul can claim that he or she has explored every avenue of the campus even after 4 years in this place. Having a self-contained campus in the outskirts of the city, away from all hustle-bustle is a vivid experience in itself. From having our own airstrip , an actual Wind Tunnel, and Olympic-sized swimming pool to one of the best academic facilities in India, the campus has a lot to offer. The lifestyle of students at IIT Kanpur coupled with the freedom we get here is unparalleled; there are no hostel deadlines and no attendance issues for classes in most of the first year courses, to mention a few.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
If you are a night-crawler, then IIT-K is the best place for you! The night-life here is inimitable. Sitting on the rooftop in New SAC or the Open Air Theatre (OAT), which witnesses frequent movie and match screenings, time flies by as hours turn to minutes under the mesmerizing starlit sky. For all you foodies out there, do not worry, for enough bait is available for you too! You will not find yourself sleeping until you see the first rays of dawn, playing all kinds of games with your friends, or perhaps some project-work or workshop you’re involved in might keep you up, or just the regular gossip and talking, or maybe during the mid semester or end semester examinations, pulling all-nighters to study in the library.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
It is always a mammoth task to make people feel right at home at a new place, specially those who have had little or no hostel experience before. The Counselling Service at IIT-K ensures you get a home away from home here. You instantly become a part of a family as soon as you enter, with your student guide! Not only does it ensure that no one is homesick, but also creates an everlasting bond with seniors and most importantly with fellow batch-mates. The junior-senior interaction is at its best, among-st all the IITs. We can vouch for that, any-day.

Campus & Culture

There are certain things you might find trivial in the beginning, but as they say, in the end, it is these small things that have the biggest impact. A live example of this can be seen in the form of the HALL CULTURE at IIT-K. The hall that you are allotted to might be random at first, but it really shapes your personality. Each hall has their own Hall song and Hall anthem, a legacy of their own which defines their ideologies. Every hall is managed by the Hall Executive Committee, comprising of a bunch of self-motivated elected students, who are also the ones that pass down the culture from one batch to the other. Every hall tries to get the better of the other by proving its mettle. You can find out more about this through other Quora answers we’ve attached as links in the end.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
One of the most interesting aspects of life here is the General Championship. An all-round the year series of events comprising of sports (Inferno), technical (Takneek) and cultural (Galaxy) competitions. All the hostels are divided into different pools, who compete for the ultimate glory! The level of these events is more than perhaps what some colleges might have in their college fests. These are a great learning experience, especially for freshers like you, giving you a view to the real world.

Yet another aspect about life here is our interesting lingo. This idea might seem alien to you right now, but once you become a part of the family, a few words will get engraved in every piece of your being and might give you a hard time while explaining, when they accidentally come out in front of your family and friends.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
Being a fresher has its own perks. Well, there may be some restrictions too, and all for good! Freshers are recommended NOT to bring their laptops in their first year. Every hall (hostel) has their own computer centre (available for use 24x7). The main Computer Centre and the New Core Labs are open till 2 am in the night, and are good enough for all practical purposes. Additionally, freshers should explore their interests, rather than wasting their time on laptops, gaming and watching movies / T.V. shows. First year is the time you discover yourself, and become independent. With so much to learn and adjust to, it is better not to shift focus towards distractions! Moreover if someone really requires a laptop urgently, a student can always contact seniors for help!

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
College Fests

Finally, here comes a tit-bit about the most thrilling and intriguing part of your stay at IIT-K: the college fests Antaragni, Udghosh and Techkriti.

Antaragni (meaning the FIRE WITHIN), the annual inter-college cultural festival of IIT Kanpur, is held every year in the month of October/November. With young bodies tapping to the beats of music in complete zeal, and hearts singing to every note of your soul, with art bringing out the deepest of emotions and the glitter of big-name concerts, Antaragni has all it takes to mesmerize even the forever-solitude-loving species on campus. The exposure you get is unmatched, and the fun, all the more exceptional.

Every year, in the month of October, the students of the institute organize a sports festival, called Udghosh. Over the years Udghosh has become a national platform where the youth of the nation get together and interact and indulge in a fair game. Udghosh witnesses participation from students from over 200 top colleges from all over India.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
Techkriti, the inter college technological and entrepreneurship festival organized by the students of IIT-K, currently enjoys the reputation of being one of the largest college fests in India, which is inspiring innovation and development in the field of science. Be it the highly awaited talks by the brightest minds, or the robo-wars attracting a swarm of people, or simply the exhibition of latest gadgets and developments, that can haunt your dreams for the next few days if you believe in the magic of science and technology, Techkriti will leave you mesmerized!

Students’ Gymkhana

Just like these fests, the student Gymkhana, a body comprising of various clubs, both cultural (music, fine arts, dramatics, dance, etc) and technological (programming club, robotics club, electronics club, etc), is run entirely by the students here. There are also many other interesting clubs and hobby groups like the card and board games club, and the adventure club, etc. You cannot find a single soul not engaged in some activity or the other. Also, sports are encouraged a lot here at IIT-K, be it rough outdoor sports like football, or the leisure sports like pool, or the articulate skating; we have all that a sports-enthusiast would crave for.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iit Kanpur A Freshmans Insight]
In the end, there is only one thing left to say. You never really leave IIT-K. Some part of you will always remain, wandering in the corridors, watching the stars from the skywalk, or sitting in the lecture halls, even after you’re here no more. IIT-K welcomes you to have a roller-coaster ride, with lots of surprises. Make your own stories! Set an example! And most importantly, make a wise choice!

In case you have further queries, feel free to contact us on Gmail, or find us on Facebook:
Krutika Kalkal: krutikalkal@gmail.com
Kunal Kapila: kunalapila@gmail.com Kunal Kapila | Facebook

Cheers!

=== That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/IITK-s-Sci-Tech-Teams
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Indian Institutes of Tech...; Student Life (general); Robotics; Teams and Teamwork; Mentors and Mentoring; Extracurricular Activitie...; University Clubs; Campus Life; Engineering Teams; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 18.
Teams and Teamwork
Mentors and Mentoring
University Clubs
Engineering Teams

Paras Mittal
Manager at Outreach Cell, IITK (2020–present)6y
IITK’s Sci-Tech Teams

Hey there! How have you been? The fact that you are reading this implies that you’ve finally qualified one of the toughest examinations in this country. If my speculation is correct, then you have my heartiest congratulations! If not, then I would say that it is just the beginning, you might have lost one opportunity, but if you look out, that was just a bucket full out of the ocean of opportunities that life has to offer.

“Ek baar IIT pahuch jao, fir sab chill hai”

They told us time and again. Well, this cannot be denied to some extent. IITs do open up a plethora of opportunities to explore. On your way to this article, you might have come across others describing the success stories of many alumni that graduated from these prestigious institutes. You might have pictured yourselves in their place, writing articles, or being written about. Then a question might have popped into your mind:

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
“How do I get from where I am to where I wanna be?”.

The crazy part is, all you need to do is to begin this journey, to take the first baby steps, you’ll find yourself running even before you know it.

“Opportunities are earned by experience, and experience is gained from these opportunities.”

I still remember the time I approached a senior from team Humanoid during IIT Kanpur’s SnT Pavilion (an exhibition of student projects of IITK) and shot questions at him - how could I join the team, what was the procedure for it? When will the recruitments take place? What are the prerequisites? I didn’t have any experience in robotics at all, was that going to make things harder for me? The answer he gave me was simple. “Chill hai (the first thing you’re gonna hear when you step on the campus), you don’t need to know anything that you haven’t learned till now”. And indeed that was the case. A very well-known quote is prevalent in the gymkhana -

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
“The only prerequisite is Enthu” (Enthusiasm)

Soon I caught myself saying that to my juniors :p. All of this began the moment I pressed the submit button in the form of nominations to join an SnT team. (Science and Technology, Ya IITians love using their slang). Stay with me. We are finally getting to the business :p.

At present, we have 6 Teams recognized by IIT Kanpur’s Science and Technology Council. Being recognized by the council means that they get funding for their projects, (Yes we need funding, the toys we play with are expensive xD) and come directly under the Student’s Gymkhana. The six teams are Humanoid, Aerial Robotics, IITK Motorsports, VISION, ERA, and AUV, out of which 5 work on projects that can be categorized as robotics. No need to worry if you aren’t interested in robotics, IITK has something for everyone who dares to look ;).

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
Humanoid is the team I started my journey with. Who doesn’t want to have a personal Iron Man suit for themselves!! Admit it- that has been a childhood fantasy of most of us :3. The vision of the team remains to develop a socially and emotionally intelligent human-like robot (that’s a dream not too far for humanity.) Team Humanoid remains the only team from India to qualify and participate in FIRA Hurocup 2019 held at South Korea and stay in the top 10 standings. (Ony 15 teams from all across the globe get selected for the prestigious humanoid-sports competition.) We certainly aim to win the next one ;).

Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/HumanoidIITK

IITK motorsports: Breaking the speed limits since 2011, this team aims at building a complete ‘racecar’! The resolve was to complement the theoretical knowledge gained in the classroom and emphasizing the practical aspects of engineering. It was such a revolutionary idea that the team started with over 150 student members, commendable, isn’t it!?

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/SAE.IITK

Aerial robotics works to develop highly efficient, autonomous UAVs (Unmanned Aerial Vehicles). Established in 2018, this team is new but ambitious, having triumphantly qualified for the IMAV 2019 held in Madrid. (Again, only 20 teams from all over the world were selected!) (exactly! In the very first year of their establishment!). They are working on some really novel ideas, such as developing a police drone system and researching ideas for increasing crop yield with help from aerial bots. The team also secured the Gold Medal in DRDO’s UAV Fleet Challenge in Inter IIT Tech Meet 8.0.

Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/ariitk

VISION(previously known as Team IGVC) works progressively on developing the best autonomous robot. They bagged the 2nd position in the Grand Challenge among 37 international teams and 3rd in the Autonomous Navigation challenge in the 27th International Ground Vehicle Challenge(IGVC) held in the year 2019. The made its debut with 5th position in the 2018 IGVC design challenge. The dream of a perfect self-driving car is about to come true!

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/vision.iitk

ERA(Équipe de Robotique Autonome) has but one aim in mind: to participate in prestigious international competitions on autonomous robotics. They have some great achievements registered under their name.

Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/eraiitkanpur

AUV(Autonomous Underwater Vehicle) works on the R&D of autonomous marine robots. Established in 2014, it currently has 2 self-developed autonomous underwater robots - Varun and Anahita. Their paper "Design and Development of an Open Frame AUV: Anahita" was presented at the IEEE OES Autonomous Underwater Vehicles Symposium held at Porto, Portugal.

Facebook: https://www.facebook.com/auviitk

All these teams originated in the form of some project that was proposed by IITK students, for IITK students. It was the initiatives taken by these students that have transcended into a blooming Sci-Tech Culture at IITK. Got something unique on your mind? Feel free to contact the clubs with your ideas, maybe someday you might look back and say - Hey, I am a co-founder of that team!

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]
These teams and all are fine, but what do you get by working with one? Well, apart from the foreign trip fully funded by IITK, you surely gain a lot of experience in both technical and management aspects - managing time, managing resources, managing people, managing events, and so on. All this, right from your first year.

For me, one crucial aspect that I got to learn was teamwork. All the new members are assigned mentors to teach them the work we do in the teams, which means that you get to learn a lot of technical skills as well. Once you are a senior member, you get to mentor your juniors and speaking from experience, that is where you can get ideas that you could never think about otherwise. Being a mentor forces you to think about things from someone else’s perspective, and that is an experience that always remains with you. The very first time I got to mentor someone was when we recruited new members for our team. Since then, I have never left such an opportunity. I had always preferred being solo, teamwork was always hard for me, but the moments I shared with my teammates taught me the importance of teamwork. You can never say that you’ve had enough, as for me, I’m still learning.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Iitk S Sci Tech Teams]

=== That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/Is-IIT-Kanpur-the-worst-IIT
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Sports; Higher Education in India; Ragging; Festivals; Alumni; Research; Indian Institute of Techn...; Campus Life; Academics; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 22.

Neil Shirude


A well-framed answer to an ill-framed question! #ThatsIITK

Prateek Yadav
Studied JEE Adv '17-AIR-2197, BITSAT '17- 399/450, KVPY' 16 Scholar & Manipal-MUOET 2017 AIR-100, CBSE- 97.2%, Kanpur 2nd Topper in CBSE Boards at Delhi Public School Azaad, Nagar KanpurUpdated 8y
Is IIT Kanpur the worst IIT?

Yes, it is. Never aim for joining it. It is the worst college of India. It’s not even worth of being a part of the IIT fraternity. I don’t know why people are crazy about joining this poor institute and study so hard just for joining IITK. Neither do I know why the government gives so much fund to this poor institute which is not at worthy of so much funds.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
I don’t understand what type of question JEE aspirants have started asking on Quora. Instead of aiming to join such a great college and studying hard to enter into this small paradise in this huge world, you are wasting time asking such questions which have no sense. Neither will asking such type of questions decrease the reputation of this institute of national importance nor would it affect anyone else’s views on IITK.

Leaving the sarcasm behind, let me come to the reality.

Source:Internet

IITK is one of the best colleges in India(rather the best). I would suggest you to aim for entering into such a college which would change your life forever. It would give you a very positive direction.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
Why I feel this question has been asked is due to the recent ragging incident and the expulsion of 22 students of IITK from the institute regarding this matter. But, my friend, one ragging incident can never ever decrease the stature of this great institute. One ragging incident can’t take away the great legacy of this institute. One ragging incident can’t mask the accomplishments of IITK.

I wish everyone should work very hard to enter into this college so that he/she gets a chance to live the amazing IITK life.

Let me enumerate the recent achievements of IITK to show you why IITK is “the worst IIT”.

Have you heard the name of Bharat Ratna C.N.R. Rao. He was once a Professor at IITK. So, this makes IITK worst because one of its faculty members receives Bharat Ratna.

Source: Google Images

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
If you are a serious IIT aspirant, you must surely have read Concepts of Physics by HC Verma. He was a Professor at IITK and retired from here just few months back. Moreover, he is also an alumnus of IIT Kanpur itself.

Source: Google Images

If you pursue computer sciences in future, AKS Primality test algorithm may be of your use. This algo has won many awards. It was built by Faculties of IITK. You may check more about this on Wikipedia AKS primality test - Wikipedia. The trio who created the AKS test also won the 2006 Gödel Prize and the 2006 Fulkerson Prize.
26 faculty members of this great institute have won the prestigious SS Bhatnagar Award. The Shanti Swarup Bhatnagar awards, first constituted in 1958, are the country’s most coveted recognition for scientists and are given annually by the Council of Scientific and Industrial Research (CSIR). Among the IITs, scientists from IIT-Kanpur were awarded seven times over the last decade, followed by IIT-Madras (4), IIT-Delhi (3), IIT-Kharagpur (2) and IIT-Bombay (1).
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]

Source: Google Images

Ashoke Sen, winner of the prestigious Fundamental Physics Prize and currently a Professor at HRI Allahabad, is an alumnus of IITK. He also is the Morningstar Visiting professor at MIT and a distinguished professor at the Korea Institute for Advanced Study. As of July 2012, the Fundamental Physics Prize is the most lucrative academic prize in the world and is more than twice as big as the amount given to the Nobel Prize awardees. This prize is also dubbed by the media as the 'XXI Century Nobel'. (Source: Wikipedia)

Coming to facilities at IITK.

There are innumerable facilities at IITK. So, I will list only the major ones.

SPORTS-

IITK has a great sports culture inside the campus and one of the best sports facilities among all Indian institutes. It is a sportsperson's paradise in it's true sense. Offering a plethora of sporting activities it ensures that students develop a versatile personality. The facilities available for each sport are top notch and could make a sport's lover's eyes glow with excitement. Be it the superb indoor squash and badminton courts, the swimming pool, which is Olympic size, or the two Gyms available to all campus residents.

New SAC, IITK

Olympic size swimming pool, 5 wooden Badminton courts(alongwith 1 badminton court in each hostel), 2 Cricket grounds, 1 Athletics field, Football and hockey fields with floodlight facility, 4 wooden Squash courts, 2 Gyms, Weightlifting room, 3 central Basketball courts(alongwith 1 basketball court in each hostel),1 indoor basketball court, 3 central Volleyball courts, Wall climbing wall and equipments(unique facility of IITK), Skating Rink, 6 synthetic Tennis courts, Yoga and Aerobic hall, Pool tables in the new Student Activity Center, Multipurpose indoor gym with Table Tennis facilities(alongwith table tennis facility in each hostel)
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
The facilities about to be setup include permanent horse riding facility, indoor olympic size swimming pool, shooting range and archery club.[2]

IITK has also won three consecutive Inter-IIT Sports Meet (2013, 2014, 2016 )(2015 Inter IIT was cancelled due to floods in Madras).

ACADEMICS-

IIT Kanpur has a strong research orientation. The diversity of research projects undertaken is a measure of IITK's strong track record of research. You may check the research highlights at https://www.iitk.ac.in/new/list-of-research-highlights
The institute has one of the best(or rather the best) academics curriculum among all IITs.
National Wind Tunnel Facility (NWTF) in IITK houses the most versatile and efficient wind tunnel in India. It has various simulation and measurement systems, interchangeable test sections and is capable of testing at wind speed up to 80 m/sec.
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
IITK has one of the best Biological Sciences, Computer Science, Physics, Aerospace, Chemistry, Mathematics and computing, and Electrical departments in India.
IITK houses three powerful supercomputers and the fourth one is planned to be installed in the institute.
SIIC (SIDBI Innovation and Incubation Centre) offers a whole gamut of incubation facilities and services to prospective entrepreneurs and intrapreneurs to convert their innovative ideas into commercially viable products. SIIC incubates ventures in technology, engineering and other interdisciplinary areas. Over the years, the center has grown tremendously and has emerged as a prestigious incubator in India (winner of the National Award for Technology Business Incubators for the year 2011). Today, it boasts of a thriving ecosystem, conducive for the growth of startups, which comprises Eight Incubation centers, World class Infrastructure, Excellent support services including the mentor and funding support, Student-run clubs and activities, Events and networking opportunities, IP and technology transfer cell.[3] IIT Kanpur has also been selected to receive the MeltingPot2020 Innovation Award 2017 under the category, Exemplary Incubation Ecosystem – Public University.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
MISCELLANEOUS

A team of students, under the guidance of IITK professors and ISRO scientists, made India’s first Nano-satellite-JUGNU, which was successfully launched from Satish Dhawan Space Center on 12 October, 2011.

JUGNU(Nano-satellite)

IITK is the only institute in India to have an airstrip[4] of its own. It also has a Gliding and Soaring Centre which offers joy rides at a nominal fee.
It is also the only institute in India to have its own Planetarium. Yes!! A planetarium inside an institute. We are proud to have our own in-house planetarium built entirely by students. The planetarium was inaugurated on Jan 29th, 2012, and still stands to marvel the dedication of the students who built it. Today we host various planetarium shows, some of which are made by the students themselves.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Is Iit Kanpur The Worst Iit]
(Student-made Planetarium at IITK)

Astronomy Club, IITK, has built its own observatory(Observatory for Astronomical Research ) . It hosts a mammoth 14 inch Celestron EdgeHD, which brings the Heavens down to earth. It houses a very high power telescope(A telescope of the same specifications is used by Stephen Hawkings !!) housed in a student-made dome. The observatory was inaugurated in summers'14. It is currently open for observations.

Observatory for Astronomical Research

The SAE club of IITK was the 2nd best Indian team in its debut participation (FSAE Italy 2013). It also won the Best Incoming Team trophy in Baja SAE 2015. Its car also won the distinction of being the India’s lightest race car in FORMULA BHARAT 2016.

IITK also has a separate team working on it ambitious project(HuRo) Humanoid Robot. Advancing towards the goal, the team worked on a new and elaborated mechanical design. Presently, the bot can walk, auto-balance, detect human faces, track objects and understand voice commands.

(BIPED by Y13)

IIT Kanpur is the first amongst all the IIT's to own a community radio service of its own. The Media Technology Centre ensures that the faculty, staff, and students utilize the facility provided by the centre and takes an active interest in the growth of information and technology. [5]

FESTS-

Antaragni- It is the annual cultural festival of IIT Kanpur, held in the month of October. It is the largest cultural festival of North India. It is one of the best college cultural fests of India.The festival also aims to serve as a platform for the expression of youth opinion on social problems. Antaragni has included performances by Amit Trivedi, Vishal Shekhar, Sonu Nigam, Shankar–Ehsaan–Loy, Poets of the Fall, Sunidhi Chauhan, Raghu Dixitand Adnan Sami. Is IIT Kanpur the worst IIT?

Yeah, IIT (Kanpur) is surely the worst IIT.

Indeed, you are 99% correct…( No sarcasm)

Go through the points as well as sources for validity

Infrastructure and building- It has a beautiful 1050 acre campus s is famous for large Peacock population which will make your day.

Does any other campus have an AIRSTRIP of its OWN in India!

We have P. K. Kelkar Library One of the biggest libraries in India

Closing rank- To grab a seat in CSE or Electrical branch you need a rank under 1000.

=== That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/Jeopardy-of-making-the-right-choice
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: College and University Ad...; Career Advice; Computer Science Engineer...; Academic Flexibility; Campus Life; Internships; Undergraduate Admissions; Aerospace and Aeronautica...; Student Life (general); 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 30.
College and University Ad...

Rahul Meena
Jeopardy of making the right choice.

Hi guys!

Let me introduce myself, I am Rahul Meena, third year undergraduate student from the Department of Aerospace Engineering at IIT Kanpur.

Congratulations on clearing JEE Advanced, each one of you have made your friends and families proud (some people jealous too probably) on achieving such a feat. Don’t be disappointed in yourself for that one question or a silly error you did, it is done. You have done a great job. It is time to move forward. Your years of struggle are finally over and your hard work has paid off, it is indeed a time to celebrate.

However, facing you now stands the challenge of choosing your future college where you will spend the next few years of your life exploring a variety of things which will undoubtedly have a big impact in shaping the rest of your life. Choosing your future college and branch is a more difficult task than clearing one of the toughest exam on the planet that is JEE. Don’t panic, we have all been there, literally. I am writing out my experience so as to help you steady your mind and make a well thought decision in this regard- know that almost everyone faces the same fears and doubts as you do in this moment, and we turned out fine.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice]
Around about two years ago, I was in the same place as yours with a burden of choosing a branch and an IIT for myself. Burden, because I had absolutely little or no knowledge about the various branches and IITs. I filled in my choices depending on the minimal knowledge that I had of the various branches. To tell you a little more about myself, ever since I was in 8th standard, I was introduced to the world of programming. By the time I cleared JEE, I was in love with programming. For the sole love with programming, I filled in CSE and MnC branches as the top choices in the choice filling.

I was awarded Aerospace Engineering at IIT Kanpur in my first as well as final round of counselling. Two years down the line, I do not regret getting this choice even a little bit and it’s not because things have changed. I still love programming and do not have much interest in my branch. For that matter, had I taken up CSE branch in some college, I would have regretted it big time. Let me tell you the reason for this, Computer Science Engineering has huge chunk of mathematics courses and Maths is just not my thing which also I surprisingly discovered after coming to college. So if you think CSE is just about sitting in front of computer and making some cool games and apps, that is not it. I was good at Maths during my JEE days, Maths during JEE and maths in college are two entirely different things. So be aware of the exact things you will be studying while choosing a branch, ask as much as you to can to as many people as you can.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice]
The flexibility at IIT Kanpur was a huge bonus for my interest. Firstly, the course template of every student here is designed in such a manner that he/she can easily take up 6–7 or more elective courses, which are basically courses of your choice in the department of your choice. Read the article on Academic Flexibility by Kunal Kapila to get a detail about that. These can be courses of any department as per your interest. I took courses of the CSE department which match my interest and reciprocate my love for programming, and plan to do so in coming semesters as well. Secondly, the professors do not refuse to provide that extra hand of support for passionate students. For instance, every now and then I reach out to other departments’ professors for projects, reading materials of my interest, and proper guidance. You just need to be enthusiastic and passionate about your work, there is a lot of help available, from seniors and professors.
Aerospace and Aeronautica...

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice]
The student culture at any college is a vital aspect in terms of the development of a student in college. When I need advice on anything, I can always find a senior who has, at one point, been in that same situation as me. As you might have heard, you cannot afford to make all of the mistakes in life by yourself. You must learn from other people’s mistakes and experiences. For example, I wasn’t aware as to how I should move forward with things so as to build a career in the field that interests me. I found couple of seniors who were from the same department as me, had the around about the same Cumulative Percentile Index (CPI) as me and the same interest as me. Isn’t that awesome? There are regular projects, lecture series and hackathons ongoing in campus which place no restriction bar on the department of the student or any other detail.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice]
During Internship and Placement sessions, there are companies which open up only for the Computer Science and Electrical students- areas I am interested in, but there are numerous companies which open up for all departments that also give me similar opportunities. For a matter of fact, the latter is greater in number as compared to the former. There are cases where a student from department other than CSE, MnC or EE will land up with a better placement or intern as compared to CSE, MnC or EE students.

To be honest, there are a lot of cases of this and that. The important thing you must remember is that even if you choose something and have doubts about it, doesn’t mean you chose wrong, or it will destroy your life. This is your chance to explore your life and make something useful out of the struggles that you might be facing. Infact, all your life you have just been preparing for this and that, once this choice of branch is made, you won’t be preparing, you will be moulding, your dreams and your future. It is all in your hand, no branch or company is more important than knowing that you are capable of doing things to change your life now, don’t be afraid of having a few setbacks, that’s what life's all about.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Jeopardy Of Making The Right Choice]
So, before you submit the final list of choices of colleges and branches, do keep this one point in mind. If you are not sure of what your interest lies in or cannot get a branch of your interest at your rank, then IITK has the best possible environment for you to pursue your interests. There will be opportunities and experiences that you will never forget. Keep a steady mind and don’t fret about making the ‘right’ choice. You are capable of doing a lot more than you think. All the best.

=== That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Kanpur By Pankaj Kumar ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/Material-Science-and-Engineering-IIT-Kanpur-by-Pankaj-Kumar
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Higher Education; Iiit Kanpur; Placement Opportunities; Materials Science and Eng...; Career Advice; Interdisciplinary Studies; Engineering; Dual Degree; Research; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 15.

Nikhil Vanjani

Studied at CSE,IIT KANPUR10y
Material Science and Engineering @ IIT Kanpur ~ by Pankaj Kumar

(B.Tech-M.Tech Dual Degree student)

Hi prospective IITians! Congratulate yourself! You have walked the proverbial ‘stair of swords’. And you know the journey doesn't end here. You have to choose a still new path. And believe me ‘to choose’ is not an easy at this point. You would have a lot of doubts. And that is normal.

In order to facilitate an informed choice, I would like to unveil some of the peculiarities of the Department of materials science and engineering at IIT Kanpur (MSE@IITK). MSE@IITK has been revered as the one of the most contributing departments towards the overall ranking of the institute. Apart from the world-class facilities and labs, we enjoy the flexibility of the course curriculum; more liberal than most of the other departments.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Ka]
The fact that the department is not that common to the engineering colleges as the other regular departments; alludes an unclear perspective (or uncertainty) of the prospects of choosing the same. This was true in my case as was for my batch mates. It starts due to the lack of availability of the right knowledge of facts and figures. I would therefore like to enlighten you of the breadth of the interdisciplinary department.

Research prospects

The department offers one of the most versatile and exhaustive areas of research namely; Biomaterials, Computational Materials Science, Process modelling, Electroceramics, Extractive Metallurgy, Flexible and Organic Electronic, Manufacturing Processes, Nanomaterials and Nanotechnology and Powder Metallurgy.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Ka]
It was to my surprise that one can actually do a research project at the end of first year itself. The professors cooperate with the students and motivate them to explore the field of research. The fact that the department is interdisciplinary leaves the student a number of labs to try his hands on. You could have you own processed Graphene or Carbon Nano tubes sheets in the second year itself. And believe me, it feels awesome! There is a friend of mine who had fabricated a thin-film transistor under the guidance of professor involved in the Flexible and organic electronics group; the privilege even a masters student seldom receive in most of the prestigious institutes.

The department offers the opportunity of converting the B tech degree to Dual Degree for the research enthusiasts; the flexibility of taking the decision remains even till the end of 7th

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Ka]
semester (just before the placement season). In essence, I would maintain the department provides you ample opportunity to explore your field of interest, before deciding your career. You never know your interest. You explore yourself in the process. I can cite enough examples on request.

Placement Prospects

The flexibility in the terms of courses we select as open electives right from the fifth semester, lets you decide your personal career path(s). Though there are limited names for the core companies, the deserving ones have been placed rightly in the core companies like the Tata motors etc. You can always target a company and select your courses accordingly. I, for example, was more into the semiconductors and energy materials and have done four dedicated courses in the field; could apply comfortably to the companies like Global Foundries, Taiwan Semiconductors etc.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Ka]
The non-core placements constitute the major section. You can build up your profile accordingly during the seven semesters. The courses are less demanding; leaves enough time for extra-curricular activities while maintaining a decent CGPA (mind it is important). The electives from the department of economics, MBA and CSE adds the perfect credentials for the most sought after companies like the PwC Diamond etc.

Cross department degree

IIT Kanpur allows you to go for a change of branch to other department (with the restrictions of minimum CGPA; of course). Or you can apply minors in the department of your interest. Apart from these, a double major program is always at your disposal.

In summary, MSE@IITK can lend you ample options for a life of your dream; be it research or otherwise.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Material Science And Engineering Iit Ka]
All the best.

=== That's IITK article: Materials Science And Engineering At Iit Kanpur ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/Materials-Science-and-Engineering-at-IIT-Kanpur
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Materials Science and Eng...; Career Avenues; Research; IIT KANPUR (Mechanical); Undergraduate Studies; Internships; Extracurricular Activitie...; Imprint; Biomaterials; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 36; comments: 8.

Richa Agrawal
Materials Science and Engineering at IIT Kanpur

Hello folks!

Here I will touch up upon the various aspects of Materials Science and Engineering (MSE). MSE at IITK is a newer branch which includes but is not limited to Metallurgical Engineering. Materials Science, as I understand is a bridge between Physics (of materials, on nanoscale) and engineering streams like Mechanical, Biological and Electrical. A materials’ scientist or a materials engineer, strives to create and perfect materials that could perform in extreme conditions, or give improved efficiency in daily life. Advancement in technology, be it spacecrafts or chips inside our computers, sensors, laser, artificial limbs, anything to everything, has been done on the base laid by materials scientists. We don’t just look at structure of materials (as you might have read in solid states chemistry) we engineer it to our advantage. We study the behavior of materials in different conditions, we engineer their structure, we mix-and-match material to decide which is best for what application. Without research in materials the entire science and technology would come to standstill, as it is us, who take the real challenge of supplying the starting raw material. Imagine a prosthetic leg, which started a wooden leg without functionality, has now become completely functional polymer leg which allowed Oscar Pistorius (survivor of double-amputations) to compete able-bodied athletes. Materials Science has paved the way for flexible electronics (imagine bending your cell phone), biomaterials (stents, prosthetics, etc), and Nanomaterials and Nanotechnology. A material scientist gets a bit of everything. We make software for analyzing materials, we do computational analysis on materials, we make electronic devices, we make bio-materials (to be used inside the human body), we make and improve structural materials (including materials for aeroplane, space-shuttles), etc. In principal we touch every branch of science possible and give it something new and extra-ordinary to work on.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Materials Science And Engineering At Ii]
Recently MHRD introduced a PAN-IIT and IISc Bangalore program IMPRINT, in which IIT Kanpur is leading the way in Materials Science domain. This might be of interest for those looking for scope in MSE: http://imprint-india.org/domains/advanced-materials

What do MSE students do during their under-graduate study?

Apart from taking up courses and giving endless quizzes and exams, students in general in IITK have an option of doing Under-Graduate Project (UGP) during which they get hands on experience of whatever they read in textbooks. We get to use advanced equipments and technology at par with any graduate or masters student here. We work on topics, and if we produce something new, we can get it published. Apart from this, during the summer break (which is 3 months long), students do an internship, either at companies or in universities across the globe.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Materials Science And Engineering At Ii]
Also, in addition to academics, IITK offers complete freedom to its students. We have a Student Gymkhana managed by the students which has various clubs and hobby groups. If you have a passion for something, we probably have a club for it. Search for Student Gymkhana IIT Kanpur, and you will know it. We plan, micro-manage and organize events (which we call festivals) whose budget runs in crores on our own. The academic load of a MSE student at IITK is relatively light, hence more freedom to indulge in extra-curricular activities, and work on self-development.

What do MSE students do after graduating?

We have endless options after graduation.

Core:

If MSE has been able to interest you, you go for core fields, but the only problem with this is that not many companies give core-jobs after B.Tech. but some which do include Tata Steel, Jindal Steel, Mitsubishi Heavy Industries, etc. So, a few of us go on for higher studies (M.Tech or PhD) and then do core jobs. Many IITK MSE alumni are studying or have graduated from top-notch institutes like MIT, Harvard, etc. After this companies like Intel, Boeing, etc absorb you or you go on to become an independent scientist.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Materials Science And Engineering At Ii]
Non- Core:

If MSE is not what you were looking for, rest assured that you will not waste 4 years of your life stuck up in wrong field. Education at IITK is relatively free. You can choose the subjects that you want to study (apart from compulsory one’s which sadly you cannot escape). There are options of doing a Minor in another branch or a double Major (you graduate with Bachelor degree in two branches), which improve your career options. If not this, then a lot of investment banks and consulting companies like Goldman Sachs, Deutsche Bank, etc. come for placements. An option of doing an MBA or UPSC is always available.

Here is a link to sum-up MSE at IITK: http://spo.iitk.ac.in/assets/docs/IIT_Kanpur_MSE_Brochure_2016-17.pdf

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Materials Science And Engineering At Ii]
Hope this helps you decide in a better way. For any more information, feel free to contact me.

=== That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At Iitk ===
Source: https://thatsiitk.quora.com/MATHEMATICS-AND-SCIENTIFIC-COMPUTING-AT-IITK
Quora page metadata - topic tags shown on the page: Career Avenues; Indian Institute of Techn...; Undergraduate Studies; Mathematics and Scientifi...; JEE Advanced 2018; Chennai Mathematical Inst...; Indian Statistical Instit...; Indian Institute of Techn...; 5 more tags not shown; upvotes: 66; comments: 22.

Shubham Prakash
MATHEMATICS AND SCIENTIFIC COMPUTING AT IITK
JEE Advanced 2018

Congratulations future IITians on your JEE Advance performance.
I am Shubham Prakash, a junior undergraduate in BS. Mathematics and Sc. Computing at IITK .
I would like to brief you all about the Mathematics and Sc. Computing Branch here.

First of all,how I landed in IITK-
Two years before,I was in same situation as yours, searching for the branches and institute which would be best for me.Since,I was fascinated with Mathematics and had a keen interest in computers ,I was confused between Maths and Computing branches of various IIT’s.Also,I was also selected for CMI and ISI, and I was in great heck. As fate would have it ,I landed here at IITK. I was happy that I landed a great place but had doubts regarding my branch.
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At]
About IITK-
I’ll focus on things unique to IIT Kanpur. The campus of IITK is much bigger than IITB and IITD even though all of them enroll about the same number of students. It’s only smaller than IITKGP however there are much more students enrolled there. We have an airstrip with WW2 gliders,Wind tunnels.Sports facilities are very good.We even have a wall climbing facility.Cultural fest ‘ANTRAGINI’ and Technical fest ‘TECHKRITI’ is worth waiting for

Secondly the academic flexibility is higher than any of the older IITS (IIT Gandhinagar exceeds Kanpur here). There are open electives and there’s no mandate to follow your template.

Branch Change policies are very flexible here.Institute tries to grants this privilege to as many students as possible under certain constraints.And this privilege is offered after 2nd,3rd and 4th semester.

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At]
Also,the institute provides an opportunity to pursue a Double Major or a Minor.
Also,if you have keen interest in your branch or any other branch, you can change your curriculum to dual degree.

IIT Kanpur believes in granting a lot of freedom to students. There are no Internet usage restrictions. You are free to roam around the campus 24*7. Men and Women are treated equally and like adults and there are no evil warden who want to ensure that people are in their rooms by bedtime.

About the Course -
It is a four year course. The curriculum provided by the institute is very flexible and open. One has ample opportunities to choose between a research oriented or a corporate career though not restricted to them. The department offers variety of courses from Abstract Mathematics to Applied Mathematics to Computer and Numerical Courses to Statistics.Since the course is offered by Mathematics Department there are more Maths courses than computer courses. Apart from this, one gets various department electives and 6 open electives which one can utilize to do courses of interest in a particular fields like Computer Science courses ( Algorithms, Database, Artificial Intelligence etc), Economics courses (Game theory, Econometric, Financial Economics etc) and Management or any other department present in the university .

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At]
Coming on the B.S. Tag, it’ not different.You are not going to be differentiated from others if you are getting a B.S. Tag. It is just a title to your course. Before joining I felt it would have some significance in the value of the course that I would complete but there is none. You will be doing all the compulsory courses a B.Tech guy will do. I feel that this doubt is created in the minds of those who are not aware. 30-40 years ago people who did a B.tech had more economic opportunities than those who did not however this course did not exist 30-40 years ago. I had a hard time explaining the difference to my parents because there is none.

Who should take this course -
Persons who have keen interest in rigorous Mathematics and enjoy logic. This program is one of the best option for people who are not sure what they want to pursue since all modern applications of most fields rely on advanced mathematics. If you adamant about pursuing Computer Science and are unfortunately not able to enroll in good program then this is the closest option you can take , but never compare

[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At]
Who should not take this course -
People who focus more on formulative approach and don’t like derivations and so. It’s a rigorous course, where one needs to focus more on concepts.

Comparisons between similar course at other places-
As I have explained about the course curriculum at IITK,I will like to focus on similar type of courses at other places.
Mathematics and Computing at IIT Delhi has more computer courses in it,but no such minors and lot of O.E.’s.Also, Computer courses are taken by Professors from Mathematics Department only.
Mathematics and Computing at IIT Guwahati focuses more on financial and computer courses than mathematics courses.They focus on applied Mathematics.
B.Math course at ISI,Bangalore has wide range over Mathematics(both analytical and applied part) .
[Context: THAT'S IITK - STUDENT ARTICLES ABOUT LIFE, ACADEMICS AND DEPARTMENTS AT IIT KANPUR > That's IITK article: Mathematics And Scientific Computing At]
Mathematics and Computer Science at CMI covers each part whether it is Mathematics or Computers, but they lack basic Engineering Courses.
I personally believe that 60-70% students don’t enjoy what they pursue and so, it’s better to choose a Bachelors course only.

Article you should also see-
A Non-Guide to JEE Counseling 2016 - by professor Dheeraj Sanghi

I also suggest you to read an article wriiten by my department seniors Akshat Mudgal and Nihar Gagarava - Mathematics And Scientific Computing , IIT Kanpur

If you have any questions, feel free to message me here or on facebook (/pshubh).
